# POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS

[← Template](../TEMPLATE.md) · [All sections](README.md)

181 distinct variants across 184 final shot prompts. Most-reused first, then by scene. Each variant links to the shot it came from.

## scene-06-03 · A young woman steps into her own apartment holding a newborn, pushes the door shut behind 

`POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS` · Wide · Handheld · 5s · **reused verbatim in 2 shots**

Used in: [scene-06-03](../shots/scene-06/03_a-young-woman-steps-into-her-own-apartment-holding.md), [unfiled-project-root-03](../shots/unfiled-project-root/03_a-young-woman-steps-into-her-own-apartment-holding.md)

````text
Exactly one adult and one newborn in frame for all 20 seconds. Nobody else enters, nobody knocks, the door stays shut once closed. The child stays in her arms throughout and is never put down. <<<mum>>> is seen in profile for the entire take and never turns to face the camera. Framing stays tight on her head and shoulders and on the bundle — the camera never widens to show the whole room, and she is never lost from frame while pacing. The camera pivots on the spot, stays handheld and alive for the full 20 seconds, never crosses to a frontal angle and never locks off. Lighting level stays constant throughout. Sharp clarity on the subject in focus, natural skin colors, no ghosting, no image flicker.

## end-credits-10 · Extreme tight 85mm shot of the elderly Japanese scientist from chest upward, three-quarter

`POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS` · Close-up · Locked-off · 5s

Used in: [end-credits-10](../shots/end-credits/10_extreme-tight-85mm-shot-of-the-elderly-japanese-sc.md)

````text
The pour happens in ONE single fast continuous motion. The bucket rotates through its full arc in roughly half a second and the ENTIRE contents leave at once as one dense mass. Fish do NOT exit one at a time, do NOT exit in sequence, do NOT trickle, do NOT pour in a stream, and are NOT spaced out across several seconds.
REAL-TIME MOTION ONLY — no slow motion, no speed ramp, no time stretch, no frame interpolation, no dramatic slowing at any point.
The rotation is fluid and continuous — it accelerates from the start of the movement through to the end with no slow initiation, no pause, no staging and no hesitation.
Frame cuts at mid-chest — never drops lower. No aquarium visible. No floor visible.
<<<prop_karp>>> bodies clearly visible above bucket rim in phase 1 — scale patterns, fins, tails readable. In phase 2 they cross the bottom frame edge together as one mass and are gone.
<<<prop_bucket>>> upper portion only in frame throughout — mouth never fully in frame when upright, handle and upper body visible.
Three-quarter profile held for entire 5 seconds — face at 45 degrees, gaze toward bucket then downward, never toward camera.
85mm locked tight — no drift. Expression calm and unchanged throughout.
<<<char_scientist2>>> identity 100% matches reference. <<<prop_karp>>> markings 100% match reference. No extra people. No subtitles. No music.
````

## end-credits-12 · A sterile white laboratory. Tight close-up of an elderly scientist laughing, the camera pu

`POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS` · Close-up · Locked-off · 8s

Used in: [end-credits-12](../shots/end-credits/12_a-sterile-white-laboratory-tight-close-up-of-an-el.md)

````text
The first frame is a TIGHT CLOSE-UP containing his WHOLE HEAD — from above the crown of the silver
hair down to just below the chin, both ears fully in frame, with a narrow margin around it. NOTHING
of the head is cropped at any edge. The head stays complete in frame until the zoom widens.
DEEP DEPTH OF FIELD for the entire shot. NO shallow focus, NO bokeh, NO dissolved background. The
man, the aquarium, the koi, the benches, the glassware, the glass divider and the rear wall are all
in readable focus at the same time. The background is always sharp and legible.
Exactly ONE zoom out, from the head close-up to a waist-up framing, occurring between 2:60 and 4:40.
No second zoom, no zoom back in, no push.
Once the camera widens, <<<char_scientist2>>> stands CENTRE-RIGHT of frame and aquarium occupies the
EXTREME LEFT, only partially visible and cut by the left frame edge. He is to the RIGHT of the tank.
The koi swim slowly and continuously inside the aquarium from the moment it enters frame until the
final frame. They never stop, never dart, never thrash, and their movement is unaffected by him.
The background is the laboratory and stays consistent with it throughout — white walls, white benches
with cabinet fronts and glassware, the vertical glass divider, the recessed ceiling panel grid, the
backlit wall panel behind the tank, the white epoxy floor.
The camera never pans, tilts, tracks or drifts. It only changes field of view. It does NOT tilt down
to follow the bow.
He is ALREADY laughing at the first frame — no still hold, no build-up.
The laugh STOPS cleanly by 2:60 and the face is fully composed before the speech.
There is a visible held beat BEFORE the line and a second visible held beat AFTER it, before the bow
begins. Nothing about this shot is rushed.
He speaks UPRIGHT, before bowing, and says exactly one line —「ありがとうございました」— and nothing else.
He bows ONCE, from the hips, back straight, to roughly thirty degrees and no deeper. His head stays
inside the frame throughout the bow and never drops out of the bottom edge. He holds at the bottom
before rising, and is still rising at the final frame.
He never looks into the lens. He never looks at the aquarium and never touches it.
Exactly one person. No one else is ever visible, no bucket, no other equipment being handled, no
duplicates. No fish outside the tank, no fish on the floor.
All movement is fluid and continuous — no jerky motion, no stop-start, no held poses mid-gesture.
Identical exposure and colour from the first frame to the last.
Sharp clarity throughout the frame, natural skin texture, no ghosting, no flickering.
````

## intro-interview-01 · An interview setup in a white laboratory.

`POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS` · Medium · Handheld · 15s

Used in: [intro-interview-01](../shots/intro-interview/01_an-interview-setup-in-a-white-laboratory.md)

````text
This is BACKSTAGE footage. It looks like the making-of camera caught the moment before and during a take — handheld, slightly loose, honest, unpolished. It must never look like the finished, tripod-mounted interview.
The first four seconds are the mic-up and are essential. He is passive and un-performing until the hands leave frame.
The recordist is present only as hands and forearms entering from screen-right. No face, no head, no full body, ever.
He speaks directly into the lens for the piece to camera, with one brief glance down at the fish and back.
The goldfish tank is beside him on the white plinth and is in frame throughout, in the lower-left. He gestures toward it once and never touches it.
Handheld throughout with continuous natural motion and two small late reframes. Never a tripod lock, never a gimbal glide, never shaky-cam.
Exactly one person on camera. Only the recordist's hands otherwise. No other crew visible, no reflections of crew in the glass partitions or the tank.
No slate, no clapperboard, no timecode burn, no camera UI, no rec dot, no on-screen text, no subtitles, no captions.
Nothing exists in the room that is not in <<<loc_lab>>> plus the lavalier mic and cable: no boom pole in frame, no lights on stands, no monitors, no chairs, no reflectors, no bags.
No visible brand marks, logos or legible lettering anywhere, including on the equipment and the lab instruments.
Single continuous take, exactly 20 seconds, real-time motion, no cuts.
Photoreal documentary texture. Fine grain, cold blue-green high-key palette, stable exposure across the full duration.
````

## intro-interview-02 · An elderly Japanese scientist is mic'd up by an unseen sound recordist in a white laborato

`POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS` · Medium · Handheld · 22s

Used in: [intro-interview-02](../shots/intro-interview/02_an-elderly-japanese-scientist-is-mic-d-up-by-an-un.md)

````text
EXACTLY THREE KOI IN THE TANK, IN EVERY SINGLE FRAME OF THE 22 SECONDS. Three before the cut, three after the cut, three at 0:00 and three at 0:22. The count never changes for any reason.
The three are the same three individuals throughout, with the same three markings: white with red-orange patches, white-and-grey with black blotches, solid deep red-orange with black speckling. Their markings do not change, swap or drift across the cut.
THE THREE SENTENCES AT 0:08.5–0:15.5 ARE ONE UNBROKEN UTTERANCE. No pause, no breath, no beat, no gap of any length between "wrong." and "He", or between "famine." and "The". The only closure inside the run is the final word "instead".
THE PAUSE AT 0:15.5–0:19 IS HELD IN FULL. It is not shortened, not filled, not covered by a cut. It is the only pause in the take, and the shorter runtime does not shorten it.
THE IMAGE IS RECTILINEAR THROUGHOUT. Straight lines stay straight, faces keep normal proportions, no wide-angle exaggeration anywhere in the frame or at any point in the 22 seconds.
THIS READS AS AN INTERVIEW: he is answering an unheard question, addressing an unseen person just off-lens to screen-right, at conversational volume, with a conversational eyeline he holds and departs from and returns to.
HE NEVER LOOKS INTO THE LENS after the cut. His eyes are on the interviewer, a few degrees off-axis screen-right.
BACKSTAGE footage — handheld, slightly loose, honest, unpolished. Never the finished tripod-mounted interview.
HE IS STANDING for all 22 seconds, before and after the cut. He never sits, never crouches, never leans. No stool, chair, bench or seat anywhere in the room or in any frame.
He holds his floor mark beside the plinth throughout. He does not walk, does not pace, does not leave frame, does not change position across the cut.
Exactly ONE hard cut, at 0:08, landing on the unanswered question. Everything before it is one continuous take; everything after it is one continuous take.
THE TANK IS THE RECTANGULAR GLASS TANK ON THE WHITE PLINTH — never a round bowl, never a jar, never a beaker. It stays on the plinth: never lifted, never carried, never moved, never touched.
The koi are large relative to the tank and visibly short of room.
The voice carries the settled authority of a senior academic, in fluent Japanese-accented English, clearly intelligible, never comic and never broken.
THE INTERVIEWER IS NEVER SEEN AND NEVER HEARD. No question audible, no murmur of acknowledgement, no shoulder, no shadow, no reflection, no figure at the edge of frame. They exist only in his eyeline and in the fact that he is answering.
The recordist appears only before the cut, only as hands and forearms from screen-right. No face, no head, no full body, ever.
Exactly one person on camera. No other crew visible, no reflections of crew in the glass partitions or the tank glass.
One brief glance toward the tank and back at 0:05. One brief downward eye departure inside the run, under a second, returning to the interviewer.
The tank is in frame in the lower-left for the full 22 seconds.
No slate, no clapperboard, no timecode burn, no camera UI, no rec dot, no on-screen text, no subtitles, no captions.
Nothing in the room that is not in the location reference plus the plinth, tank, lavalier and cable. No stool, no bucket, no food container, no boom pole in frame, no lights on stands, no monitors, no reflectors, no bags.
No visible brand marks, logos or legible lettering anywhere.
Photoreal documentary texture, fine grain, cold blue-green high-key palette, stable exposure across the full 22 seconds.
````

## intro-interview-03 · An elderly Japanese scientist, mic'd and standing in a white laboratory, finishes an answe

`POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS` · Medium · Handheld · 13s

Used in: [intro-interview-03](../shots/intro-interview/03_an-elderly-japanese-scientist-mic-d-and-standing-i.md)

````text
THE CAMERA NEVER PANS AND NEVER TILTS. One framing, held for all 14 seconds. No swing, no rise, no drop, no reframe, no recompose, no adjustment to the subject. What is in frame at 0:00 is in frame at 0:14. The only camera movement is involuntary handheld micro-drift and breathing float that always returns to the same framing.
No push-in, no zoom, no dolly, no truck, no crane, no orbit, no rack focus.
THIS READS AS AN INTERVIEW: he is finishing an answer to an unheard question, addressing an unseen person just off-lens to screen-right, at conversational volume.
HE NEVER LOOKS INTO THE LENS, including on the final line and in the hold after it.
BACKSTAGE footage — handheld, slightly loose, honest, unpolished. Never the finished tripod-mounted interview.
The take BEGINS MID-WORD, first syllable clipped. No lead-in, no beat before he speaks.
THE THREE-SECOND BEAT at 0:04.5–0:07.5 IS HELD IN FULL. It is not shortened, not filled, not covered, and nothing is added under it.
The final line is delivered flat and evenly, at the same conversational level as everything else. It is not a reveal, not a threat, not a punchline. No vocal drop, no lean-in, no change of expression, no emphasis on any single word.
Nothing marks the final line: no camera move, no lighting change, no sound, no reaction from the fish, no change in his face.
HE IS STANDING for all 14 seconds. He never sits, never crouches, never leans. No stool, chair, bench or seat anywhere in the room or in any frame.
He holds his floor mark throughout and does not drift out of the fixed framing. He does not walk, does not pace, does not leave frame.
NO RECORDIST, no hands, no forearms, nobody in the foreground at any point.
THE TANK IS THE RECTANGULAR GLASS TANK ON THE WHITE PLINTH — never a round bowl, never a jar, never a beaker. It stays on the plinth: never lifted, never carried, never moved, never touched.
EXACTLY FOUR koi in the tank, visible throughout, matching the four markings in the fish reference. Never three, never five, never a shoal. The koi are large relative to the tank and visibly short of room.
````

## intro-stock-01 · A vote has just closed in a large international assembly chamber.

`POSITIVE CONSTRAINTS` · Wide · Crane / high angle · 15s

Used in: [intro-stock-01](../shots/intro-stock/01_a-vote-has-just-closed-in-a-large-international-as.md)

````text
Standard-definition interlaced broadcast video, not film. Visible scanlines, light combing on moving edges, soft low-contrast picture, faded colour with magenta-shifted whites, mild chroma smear on saturated reds and blues, blooming on the overhead fixtures, compression macroblocking in the darker upper rows.
One brief horizontal tracking wobble crosses the whole frame at 0:09 and resolves within half a second. Very slight continuous horizontal jitter under every shot. Video texture is identical across all four shots.
Palette is beige, dull gold, grey-blue, greenish fluorescent. Desaturated and institutional.
The display board stays blank and dark throughout. No readable text anywhere in frame, no signage, no logos, no insignia, no identifiable emblems, no on-screen graphics.
Hundreds of delegates present, no single face emphasised, no duplicate people, no crowd reaction, no applause, no raised voices, no drama.
No film grain, no HDR, no modern digital sharpness, no colour grade, no shallow depth of field outside Shot C.
Stable picture, no ghosting, no strobing flicker.
````

No CCTV timestamp, no phone-video vertical framing, no rolling-shutter jelly, no scanlines, no VHS artefacts, no lo-fi degradation, no found-footage aesthetic.

No reporter in frame, no microphone in frame, no second camera crew visible.
No blood, no visible wounds, no bodies on the ground, no weapons other than shields and batons held at rest.
No duplicate people, no crowd appearing or disappearing between cuts.
Stable picture within each shot, no ghosting, no strobing flicker, no slow motion.

NOBODY IS RESOLVED as an individual: bodies, coats, hoods and scarves, lit on one side and black on the other. No face is held, no expression is read, nobody is a protagonist. NOBODY LOOKS AT THE CAMERA.
ONE EVENT ONLY: a man throws an armful of files on the fire at 0:02 and walks back out of the light. Everything else is continuous state.
NO CONFRONTATION AND NO SPECTACLE: no police, no shields, no uniforms, no weapons, no vehicles, no second group, no front, no violence, no casualties, no bodies.
NO IDENTIFIABLE AFFILIATION: no flags, no banners, no colours, no insignia, no readable markings.
NO READABLE TEXT ANYWHERE IN FRAME: nothing legible on the burning documents, no signage on the office frontage, no shop signs, no graffiti with words, no on-screen text.
No duplicate people, no repeated figures, no mirrored composition, no evenly distributed crowd, no tiling of the street or the facade.
Anamorphic 2.39:1, night, photoreal live-action capture. Naturalistic low-key firelight, real grain, grounded physical texture. No CG gloss, no game-engine look, no plastic skin, no synthetic fire, no particle sparks, no volumetric fog haze substituting for smoke.
Sharp clarity where the light allows, natural colours, no ghosting, no flickering of the image itself, no strobing, no motion smoothing.

Continuous involuntary micro-activity in her face and body for all 10 seconds — she is never a still image.

<<<char_baby>>> stays ASLEEP for the whole take. Its eyes never open. It never cries, never startles, and specifically does not cry when he appears or when she turns.
She rocks CONTINUOUSLY from the first frame to the last, at an unchanging tempo, including during the arc, the reveal, the turn and the focus change. Her thumb never stops moving on the cloth.
Her body does not react protectively: she does not clutch the baby tighter, does not shield it, does not turn away, does not stand, does not shrink back.
Her face gives almost nothing: no fear, no terror, no pleading, no defiance, no tears. Only a slowing of the blink, a slight set of the jaw, and one swallow.
Neither of them speaks. Neither looks into the lens.
The camera never pans toward the doorway, never follows him, never reframes.
No lighting change of any kind at any point.
Real-time motion for all 14 seconds. No slow motion, no speed ramp, no time stretch, no freeze.
Handheld for the whole take, never locked off and never stabilised.
The take ends on the empty doorway in focus, with her soft in the foreground, head still turned toward it, still rocking her sleeping child. Nothing resolved, nothing decided.

Their positions and eyelines follow <<<image_1>>>: he stands on the open floor beside the foot of the bed, roughly one step from her; she sits on the edge of the long side of the bed at his hip height; their gazes cross, his going down to her and hers going up to him. Nothing else is taken from that reference, and the stick figures and arrows are never rendered in any form.
THREE-QUARTER PROFILE close-up on <<<char_cap2>>>'s bare face for the entire take, roughly 60 to 70 degrees off his front, at his standing eye level. One framing, held for all 10 seconds. Never frontal, never full profile, never square to the lens. No helmet at any point.
HE LOOKS DOWN AT HER from the first frame through the word, at a steep close-range angle. He breaks the look only at 0:05, when she starts to rise, and his eyes go level and STAY level for the rest of the take. He never looks at her again after that.
His head never turns to follow her: not as she rises, not as she passes, not after she has gone.
Nothing readable passes between them while they are looking at each other: no pity, no reassurance, no apology, no plea, no thanks, no defiance, no nod, no signal.
The single word is clearly formed and clearly visible on his mouth despite the angle.
No gear marks on his face: no pressure lines, no indentations, no red bands.
EXACTLY ONE WORD is spoken in the whole take: "Go." at 0:03, in the voice of <<<audio_1>>>. It is never repeated, never extended, nothing is added before or after it, and nobody else speaks.
The delivery is flat, quiet and procedural. Not gentle, not urgent, not whispered, not growled. No emphasis, no inflection.
His face gives NOTHING at any point: no pity, no compassion, no guilt, no resolve, no relief, no anguish. The decision happened before this shot and is never shown.
HE MOVES ASIDE for her: one small half-step back with a slight rotation of the shoulders, roughly thirty centimetres, arriving slightly late. It is never a stride, never a retreat, never accompanied by a gesture, never preceded by a look. He returns to roughly his original position afterwards.
He does not usher her, does not gesture, does not nod, does not raise a hand, does not move the weapon out of her way.
<<<mum>>> is a dark out-of-focus mass in the lower frame at the start, and crosses the foreground fully out of focus between 0:05 and 0:07. She is never sharp, her face is never legible, and the baby is never legible.
She does not stop, does not look back once she has stood, does not speak, does not touch him, does not hesitate, does not run.
Focus stays on his near eye for all 10 seconds and never racks to her, never hunts, never follows.
<<<char_baby>>> makes no sound at any point.
<<<m7>>> stays at or below the bottom frame edge, muzzle down, never raised, never moved aside, never featured. Its optic and side unit are dark and unlit: no reticle, no glow, no laser, no lamp.
Neither of them looks into the lens. He never fires, never removes his gloves.
The camera does not react to the word, to his gaze change, to his half-step, or to her passing: no push-in, no pan, no follow, no reframe, no tilt.
No lighting change except the momentary darkening as her body blocks the light, the marginal darkening from his half-step, and the catchlight becoming clearer as his eyes come up off her.
Real-time motion for all 10 seconds. No slow motion, no speed ramp, no time stretch, no freeze.
Handheld for the whole take, never locked off and never stabilised.
The take ends on his face, looking level at an empty room, breathing unresolved, nothing expressed and nothing decided on screen.
Kodak Vision3 500T, naturalistic low-key cold daylight, real grain, grounded physical cinema texture, no blur, no ghosting, no flickering.

NEVER VISIBLE: his mouth, moustache, nose, cheeks, eyes, brow, forehead, hair. The camera never tilts up to find any of it. See the HEIGHT AND EYELINE block: it governs the framing and overrides anything else.
IT READS AS A MAN TOO TALL FOR THE FRAME, not as a headless body: the jaw and beard edge are present and readable at the top edge, cut cleanly by the frame line.
His hands, belt and weapon are below the bottom edge and never seen. No helmet.
HE IS STANDING AND SHE IS SEATED FOR ALL 5 SECONDS. He never crouches, kneels, sits, stoops or lowers himself; she never rises, stands or straightens to his level. The height difference is never reduced.
HER GAZE ENDS ABOVE THE TOP OF THE PICTURE: she looks steeply up and screen-right, past his visible chin, at a face that is not in the shot, and holds it there for the rest of the take. She does not lower her gaze, does not look at the child, does not look into the lens.
THE GEOMETRY FOLLOWS <<<image_1>>> AND IS NEVER FLIPPED: she sits LEFT of centre, he enters from the RIGHT and stops in the RIGHT foreground, the key comes from off-frame SCREEN-LEFT. See the SCREEN DIRECTION block.
HER POSTURE AND THE CHILD IN HER ARMS FOLLOW <<<image_2>>> exactly: the cradle, the hand on the bundle, the child high against her chest, the jacket, the fleece collar, the hair. Her head angle does not follow it: she looks far higher.
THE BACKGROUND IS A BARE PALE WALL AND NOTHING ELSE. No bed, no headboard, no window, no curtain, no lamp, no mirror, no air-conditioning unit, no door, no corner, no skirting, no ceiling, no floor, no furniture, no object of any kind.
NOTHING HAPPENS THAT IS NOT IN ACTION TIMING. No added gesture, no added movement, no added reaction, no added beat, from either character, on or off frame. See the ACTION LOCK block.
THE TAKE BEGINS WITH THE RIGHT FOREGROUND EMPTY and with HER HEAD DOWN. He is not in the first frame. Her head is not already raised.
HE ENTERS FROM THE RIGHT EDGE at 0:01, walks in upright at an ordinary pace, and STOPS in front of her by 0:02.5. He never crosses her, never occludes her face, never continues to frame left, never exits, never moves again after stopping.
SHE RAISES HER HEAD ONCE, overlapping his entrance, the head going back on the neck through a visibly long travel, and her eyes arrive at their high off-frame point as he comes to rest. No second lift, no lowering, no repeat, no overshoot, no snap, no startle.
THE FEAR IS PHYSICAL AND UNPERFORMED: shallower breathing, a locked upward gaze, a fine tremor at the jaw, visible strain in the neck, and a body that does not move.
HER BODY NEVER MOVES: she does not rise, does not shift on the bed, does not lean, does not pull the child closer, does not change her grip. Only her head moves.
NOTHING IS EVER FROZEN: continuous involuntary micro-motion on her head and on his standing column. Neither ever nods, shakes, tilts as a signal or reacts.
Her rocking continues unchanged for all 5 seconds, including through his entrance and the lift: it never slows, never quickens, never pauses.
<<<char_baby>>> stays asleep, out of focus, never touched, never adjusted, never uncovered, never looked at, and makes no sound.
She never looks into the lens. The camera is never his eyes.
FOCUS NEVER MOVES: no rack to him as he enters, no pull back to her, no hunting, no breathing focus.
The camera does not react to anything: no pan, no tilt, no push-in, no reframe, no rack focus, no change of height.
NO LIGHTING CHANGE OF ANY KIND. Her face is revealed by her own movement through a fixed source; the right side of frame goes dark only because an unlit body occupies it. No shadow sweeps across her. NO RED LIGHT ANYWHERE IN THE FRAME.
No readable text anywhere in frame: no signage, no labels, no on-screen text, no interface, no HUD.
Real-time motion for all 5 seconds. No slow motion, no speed ramp, no time stretch, no freeze. No frozen or motionless head at any point.
Handheld for the whole take, never locked off and never stabilised.
The take ends on her face, her eyes up on a man whose face we never see, nothing said, nothing changed.

He is already moving at the first frame — no static hold at the head of the shot.
He works forward in two separate movements with a settle between them. He NEVER slides, glides or
rolls smoothly. The chair has no wheels and never rolls.
The adjustment is natural and unremarkable — the ordinary way a person pulls their chair in.
His hands grip the SIDES of the chair and release afterward. His forearms enter the bottom of frame
only briefly; his hands are never fully in frame.
He composes himself at the end — back straightening, shoulders squaring, chin levelling — and holds
that to the final frame.
He never speaks, never turns toward camera, never looks into the lens, never stands up again.
His pale grey shirt stays clearly darker than the white wall behind it and never blows out to match
it. His hair and beard stay dark and readable throughout.
Handheld and imperfect throughout, reacting late to his movements rather than anticipating them.
Identical exposure and colour from the first frame to the last.
Sharp clarity on his face, natural skin texture, no ghosting, no flickering.
````
